## 2026-10-13 — Shift close receipt and pending allowance

Implemented the owner-requested shift workflow on `feature/shift-close-receipt-pending-allowance` from `origin/main` `7972d963`: cashier blind close now records only physical cash, system derives non-cash totals, submission releases operational assignment, pending and approved close sheets share canonical calculations, and open-shift creation allows up to two pending prior closes while enforcing one open shift. Added focused English/Arabic-ready UI copy and regression coverage. Focused MariaDB PASS 8/74. Fast QA ran once with 7/8 checks passing; CodeGraph initially reported the inherited main-worktree index, so a worktree-local index was initialized afterward without rerunning the gate. No migration, production write, merge or deployment. Code, tests, locale files and print/UI views changed; commit and push follow.

## 2026-10-13 — Audit 500 fix

Traced issue #9 from production logs to Livewire island compilation touching stale root-owned generated files under shared `storage/framework/views/livewire/islands`; normal writes succeeded but mtime updates failed for the FPM account. Removed compiled islands from the audit view and added a deployment-template guard that clears only generated Livewire/Blaze caches and recreates them for `rajeh`. Updated focused audit presentation/export coverage. Focused isolated MariaDB check passed 3 tests/12 assertions. Production inspection was read-only; no migration, cache mutation, service restart, merge or deployment occurred. Code and documentation changed; commit and branch push follow.

- **Task:** Improved the perceived and actual interaction feedback for the Arabic `إدارة` action on `/admin/authorization-baseline`.

- **Verification:** `php artisan view:clear`, Arabic/English JSON parsing, and `git diff --check` passed. Arabic RTL browser verification confirmed the correct Livewire target and loading bindings, modal opening in about 300 ms, no server error, and empty browser logs. No English browser check or automated tests were run. No commit or push.

## 2026-10-07 — Module 1 authentication timing mitigation portability correction

On `hotfix/module1-redteam-approvals-20261007`, replaced the tentative fixed bcrypt dummy digest after lead review showed it would not track the test/runtime cost or alternate configured hash drivers. The final `FortifyServiceProvider` correction uses Laravel 13's native `Illuminate\Support\Timebox` and the installed `auth.timebox_duration` default. It preserves the existing username lookup, active-status policy, password hash check, generic error and login limiter; failed callbacks use the minimum-duration box and valid active callbacks return early.

On only `qa_rajh_m1_approvals_20261007`, the final red-team auth check passed 6 tests / 65 assertions, including missing/inactive/active-wrong-password denial through the same configured Timebox and a real successful login return-early path. No wall-clock benchmark was run; Laravel Timebox is a minimum, so callbacks exceeding the configured duration can still take longer. No full suite, browser, production/remote data, push, or deployment.

## 2026-10-18 — Selected destination allocation UX hotfix
